MINISTRY OF FINANCE 

( Department Of Revenue ) 

( CENTRAL BOARD OF DIRECT TAXES ) 

NOTIFICATION 

New Delhi , the 14th February , 2023 

G.S.R 95 ( E ) . In exercise of the powers conferred by section 139 read with section 295 of the 
Income - tax Act , 1961 ( 43 of 1961 ) , the Central Board of Direct Taxes hereby makes the following rules 
further to amend the Income - tax Rules , 1962 , namely : 


1. Short title and commencement ( 1 ) These rules may be called the Income - tax ( Second Amendment ) 
Rules , 2023 . 


( 2 ) They shall come into force with effect from 1st day of April , 2023 . 

2. In the Income - tax Rules , 1962 , in Appendix - II , for Form ITR - 7 , the following Form shall be substituted , 
namely :
